2738 — dumbbell lying triceps extension on floor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2738 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

A floor version of the lying triceps extension, where the floor limits how far you lower the weight.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on the floor with knees bent, holding a dumbbell in each hand over your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2738.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Lie on the floor with knees bent, holding a dumbbell in each hand over your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2738.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Lie on the floor with knees bent, holding a dumbbell in each hand over your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2738.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2738.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on the floor with knees bent, holding a dumbbell in each hand over your chest. 2738.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep upper arms still and bend your elbows to lower the weights toward your head. 2738.json#/instructions/1
  3. Stop just before the weights touch the floor. 2738.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press back up by straightening your arms. 2738.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your elbows pointing up. 2738.json#/instructions/4

2740 — wide push up (wall)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2740 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A beginner-friendly standing push-up against a wall with a wide hand position to emphasise the chest.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it, wider than your shoulders.

  4. Push back to the start. 2740.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips in line with your shoulders. 2740.json#/instructions/4

2741 — ez-barbell standing wide grip biceps curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2741 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing EZ bar curl with a wide grip. Emphasises the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the ez barbell with an underhand grip, hands wider than shoulder-width apart.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2741.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the ez barbell with an underhand grip, hands wider than shoulder-width apart. 2741.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your elbows close to your torso and your upper arms stationary throughout the movement. 2741.json#/instructions/1
  3. Curl the barbell up towards your shoulders by contracting your biceps. 2741.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the barbell back to the starting position. 2741.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 2741.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2742 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A machine hip thrust with the feet raised on a step box, for a deeper glute contraction.

Proposed staging

2744 — cable standing hip thrust

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2744 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing hip thrust against cable resistance, training hip extension and the glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a belt or strap to a low cable and stand facing away from the stack. Hold the cable at your hips with feet shoulder width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2744.json#/instructions/0, 2744.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach a belt or strap to a low cable and stand facing away from the stack. 2744.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the cable at your hips with feet shoulder width apart. 2744.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hinge slightly, then thrust your hips forward. 2744.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your glutes at the top. 2744.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return slowly and keep your ribs down. 2744.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2745 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: prone.

A reverse hyperextension on the smith machine that works the glutes and hamstrings with support from the lower back.

Proposed staging

Lie face down over a bench with your hips at the edge and legs hanging.

Proposed staging
